## Ritmo
* `VisionSense`: - [LookTo|**Ritmo**] Head look like breathing rhythm if swimming front crawl
* `Strategy`: - **Ritmo**, Passag, Kontakt
* `Ritmo.md`: - Etymology(**Ritmo**)
* `Ritmo.md`: - How **Ritmo** Interacts with Pulse and Beat
* `Ritmo.md`: - **Ritmo** Animates the Pulse: The pulse provides the steady grid, while **Ritmo** provides the variety. **Ritmo** can mimic the pulse exactly, or it can move faster, slower, or stop entirely while the pulse continues silently underneath.**Ritmo**
* `Ritmo.md`: - Divides the Beat: Beats are equal units of time. **Ritmo** breaks these units into smaller pieces (like eighth notes or sixteenth notes) or stretches across multiple beats (like half notes or whole notes).
* `Ritmo.md`: - **Ritmo** Creates Syncopation: When a **Ritmo** deliberately places accents between the steady beats rather than directly on them, it creates syncopation. This friction between the unexpected **Ritmo** and the expected pulse is what makes music swing or feel groovy.

* `Strategy`: - ((Strategy + Taktik - **Plan**) = Uncoordinated Action). You know what you want to do and how to do it, but nobody knows who is doing it or when it starts.
* `Strategy`: - ((Strategy + **Plan** - Taktik) = Rigid Inflexibility). You have a goal and a schedule, but you don't know how to actually execute the maneuvers on the ground.
* `Strategy`: - ((Taktik + **Plan** - Strategy) = Busy Work). You are doing lots of things efficiently and on schedule, but you have no idea why or if it's actually helping you win.

* `Strategy`: - **Taktik**
* `Strategy`: - ((Strategy + **Taktik** - Plan) = Uncoordinated Action). You know what you want to do and how to do it, but nobody knows who is doing it or when it starts.
* `Strategy`: - ((Strategy + Plan - **Taktik**) = Rigid Inflexibility). You have a goal and a schedule, but you don't know how to actually execute the maneuvers on the ground.
* `Strategy`: - ((**Taktik** + Plan - Strategy) = Busy Work). You are doing lots of things efficiently and on schedule, but you have no idea why or if it's actually helping you win.
* `Strategy`: - When is an AKTION a **Taktik**?

## Don
* `Betaglot`: - **Don**'t just perceive the Beta as a subject, instead be the Beta because there is no subject and object. Awaken to the reality that there is no separation between the object and subject. You are non dual awareness and nothing else
* `Error-Fall`: - **Don**'t Rely on Flora since you could rip it out of the ground if there's enough force
* `Error-Fall`: - **Don**'t cross your legs, because you could LegSnag yourself
* `Error-Fall`: - **Don**'t Sit if you're gonna get impaled
* `Parry-Flora`: - **Don**'t try to parry large trees which are falling on you
* `Skim-Hand`: - Rip up a handful of Poa then throw it. **Don**'t waste your energy by throwing it far
* `Skim-Hand`: - **Don**'t throw Poa into the wind because it is light enough to fly back into your face
* `Skim-Leg`: - **Don**'t BaseSkreef Poa

* `Trench`: - [Trench|Markation] = "**Don**'t flag each tree in trenches"
* `Cow`: - **Don**'t plant in trench Nadir because cows ActualName(Tightrope walk) along the center
* `LaborOverview`: - **Don**'t take all the easy land
* `Doff`: - Opposite of **Don**
* `Doff`: - RainShell **Don** and Doff when the weather is frequently changing from Rain to \!Rain
* `Doff`: - InsulationLayer **Don**Doff when Atmosfera is variable
* `Kneel`: - **Don**'t kneel on shoulder straps
* `Sit`: - **Don**'t sit at the cache. **Don**'t get too comfortable

Besides the weight of Bags, there is no experience of a Neon unless it's in your hand or you're looking at it already planted in the land. There should never be any dropped trees to be confusing. * `Ritmo.md`: - Divides the **Beat**: **Beat**s are equal units of time. Ritmo breaks these units into smaller pieces (like eighth notes or sixteenth notes) or stretches across multiple beats (like half notes or whole notes).

## Pulse
* `Beat`: - Differentiate(**Pulse**,Beat)
* `Ritmo.md`: - How Ritmo Interacts with **Pulse** and Beat
* `Ritmo.md`: - Ritmo Animates the **Pulse**: The pulse provides the steady grid, while Ritmo provides the variety. Ritmo can mimic the pulse exactly, or it can move faster, slower, or stop entirely while the pulse continues silently underneath.Ritmo

## Syncopation
* `Ritmo.md`: - Ritmo Creates **Syncopation**: When a Ritmo deliberately places accents between the steady beats rather than directly on them, it creates syncopation. This friction between the unexpected Ritmo and the expected pulse is what makes music swing or feel groovy.

## Proximal
* `BetaScaffold`: - Zone of **Proximal** Development (ZPD): Scaffolding operates within this "optimal learning window," which is the gap between what a student can do alone and what they can achieve with expert guidance.
* `BetaScaffold`: - Lev Vygotsky: Developed the concept of the Zone of **Proximal** Development, which forms the theoretical foundation for scaffolding.
* `Aktion-Flora-Branch`: - Branch is compressed between ThumbDistalPhalanx and IntermediatePhalanges or **Proximal**Phalanges of IndexFinger and MiddleFinger

* `Strategy`: - Strategy: **Proximal** Motive, Ulterior Motive
* `Finger`: - PIP **Proximal** Interphalangeal Joint
* `ComputationalGeometry`: - **Proximal** tree

## Neosite
* `Kontakt-Spade-Shaft`: - Used when pushing and holding Flora back away from **Neosite**
* `Mikrosite-Select`: - The zone where a **Neosite** can go relative to PlantedSites
* `Mikrosite-Select`: - The side lengths of the **Neosite**Polygon.
* `Mikrosite-Select`: - You don't select a **Neosite**, a **Neosite** is a Mikrosite which has already been planted
* `Neon`: - "**Neosite**" doesn't make sense, a planted Neon is a Neon. Neons in your Bag aren't instantiated. Besides the weight of Bags, there is no experience of a Neon unless it's in your hand or you're looking at it already planted in the land. There should never be any dropped trees to be confusing. A Mikrosite which has been planted is no longer a Mikrosite for another Neon.
* `Mikrosite`: - **Neosite** doesn't include Natural Crop Trees
* `Mikrosite`: - **Neosite** is a planted Mikrosite
* `ViaOverview`: - Abandon CurrentSite for **Neosite** when you determine that CurrentSite isn't good
* `Triad`: - Triad of **Neosite**s, Stumps, Protektors
* `RelativeDirection`: - **Neosite**
* `RelativeDirection`: - **Neosite**.Markum
* `RelativeDirection`: - (Follow = RelativeTo(**Neosite**.Markum))
